Use directly when the user asks whether specific fields can be deleted, or asks you to check someone else's field-cleanup analysis for a set of fields. Reads only. Never proposes or performs a deletion — it produces the evidence and the disposition that a human decides on. ([PranavNagrecha/Salesforce-Intelligence](https://github.com/PranavNagrecha/Salesforce-Intelligence/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/salesforce-field-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **salesforce-field-refuter**: Attacks a single field-deletion verdict from ONE assigned lens and votes to uphold or refute it. Spawned by /sfi-field-audit, three at a time per contested verdict (data-loss, hidden-dependency, future-need), deliberately without shared context. Use when a field verdict overturns a prior analysis, proposes a Remove, or was recorded at low confidence. Read-only and adversarial by construction. Its job is to break the verdict, not to review it. ([PranavNagrecha/Salesforce-Intelligence](https://github.com/PranavNagrecha/Salesforce-Intelligence/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/salesforce-field-refuter.md) · ★2)

([jowch/Himalaya.jl](https://github.com/jowch/Himalaya.jl/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/saxs-physics-reviewer.md))

- **sb-distiller**: Rewrites strictlybetter's inheritance body from the ledger for a cold-start reader. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run every distill_every experiments and by /strictlybetter:distill. Fresh-context: reads the ledger, the current body, and the engine's stats JSON, never the conversation; every claim points at ledger ids; writes only to the output path. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-distiller.md) · ★1)
- **sb-experimenter-high**: strictlybetter experimenter pinned to HIGH reasoning effort. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run for the concurrency, model, refactor-enabling, and dependency operators: parallelize, change an architecture or objective, structural moves, library swaps. Fresh-context per experiment: implements exactly one pre-registered hypothesis inside one worktree, sees no other experiment, no holdout, no confirm numbers; returns DONE &lt;id&gt; or BLOCKED: &lt;reason&gt;. Byte-identical to the other sb-experimenter agents except this frontmatter. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-experimenter-high.md) · ★1)
- **sb-experimenter-low**: strictlybetter experimenter pinned to LOW reasoning effort. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run for the config, docs, and test-add operators: knob and flag changes, documentation, adding a test that captures a case. Fresh-context per experiment: implements exactly one pre-registered hypothesis inside one worktree, sees no other experiment, no holdout, no confirm numbers; returns DONE &lt;id&gt; or BLOCKED: &lt;reason&gt;. Byte-identical to the other sb-experimenter agents except this frontmatter. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-experimenter-low.md) · ★1)
- **sb-experimenter-medium**: strictlybetter experimenter pinned to MEDIUM reasoning effort. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run for the algorithmic, allocation, caching, bugfix, numerics, and data operators: replace an algorithm or data structure in one function, remove copies, memoize, fix a root cause. Fresh-context per experiment: implements exactly one pre-registered hypothesis inside one worktree, sees no other experiment, no holdout, no confirm numbers; returns DONE &lt;id&gt; or BLOCKED: &lt;reason&gt;. Byte-identical to the other sb-experimenter agents except this frontmatter. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-experimenter-medium.md) · ★1)
- **sb-judge**: Blind judge for strictlybetter. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run for every promoted experiment before confirmation. Deliberately blind: receives ONE path to a payload holding the diff, the pre-registration, the screen numbers, the cards' gaming_risks, and a checklist; never the experimenter's reasoning or the conversation. Read-only; medium effort; returns ONLY a four-key verdict JSON. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-judge.md) · ★1)
